Science 100 and Nuke/UseNuke 100 are existential priorities - atomic weapons only hope against 13k German military. Production 100 for emergency unit production. Focus: Hold cities, survive until nuclear deterrent operational.

- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Rocketry: Allows you to build the Guided Missile, a one-time use unit specialized in damaging City garrisons.
        Completing it would unlock: Nuclear Fission, Radar
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Penicillin: All Workers receive the Fallout Reduction Promotion, which reduces the amount of damage they take while scrubbing Fallout. Allows you to build the Marine, a Unit that specializes in amphibious assaults. Also allows you to build the Medical Lab, a building which speeds the growth of your Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 3
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10

        - Forgiveness: 7
        - Meanness: 3
        - Neediness: 4
        - Chattiness: 8
        - DeceptiveBias: 1
        - Rationale: Tweaked by In-Game AI (GERMAN BETRAYAL - TOTAL WAR POSTURE: Germany's surprise attack (despite defensive pact and shared Order ideology) demonstrates no alliance is safe. Increased WarBias to 9 and HostileBias to 9 for maximum military readiness against dual-threat (Germany + France). Reduced DiplomaticBalance to 8 and FriendlyBias to 6 - trust is broken. Maintained MinorCivCompetitiveness at 10 but increased MinorCivFriendlyBias to 10 - must aggressively rebuild Lisbon/Kyzyl alliances to recover delegates lost to Decolonization. Survival requires total commitment to military-industrial complex and nuclear deterrent.)
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Academy of Sciences (Policy)
        - Rationale: ACADEMY OF SCIENCES - NUCLEAR RACE ACCELERATION: Order Level 2 tenet provides 5 free Research Labs (saving ~3000 production) and +100% production to remaining labs. With 9 cities, this immediately boosts science output by 300-400 per turn, cutting Atomic Theory/Nuclear Fission research time by 5-7 turns. Alternative Dictatorship of the Proletariat (+63 yields/turn) inferior when facing imminent military collapse - need infrastructure NOW. Great Leap Forward's free tech tempting but labs provide permanent boost.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Buenos Aires
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Buenos Aires: Reset to 0/0 from previous. Culture quest (we trail 908 vs 1586) is unwinnable. Not worth resources. They're Germany's ally anyway.
        - UpdatedTurn: 167
        
        ### Sokoto
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Sokoto's Great Admiral quest has expired with Germany maintaining overwhelming influence (284 vs our 64). Unable to contest this alliance, we reset to neutral to conserve resources for winnable city-state competitions and avoid diplomatic friction with our German allies.
        - UpdatedTurn: 187
        
        ### Valletta
        - Public: 25
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Valletta (Catholic majority, Inca ally) offers potential influence gains. Moderate positivity preserves quest opportunities without provoking Inca (trade partner) or committing resources better spent on military production against French naval threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 230
        
        ### Colombo
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Colombo (Player 28, city-state): Resetting relationship from Friendship (47/-58) to neutral 0/0. We made peace with Colombo this turn (Events 27-31). Colombo is now a German sphere of influence ally. While no longer hostile, we should remain neutral as Germany controls their destiny. Preservation of German alliance is paramount - they provide our defensive pact and are our only credible military partner against France. Don't compete with Germany here.
        - UpdatedTurn: 302
        
        ### Salem
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Salem: Resetting to 0/0 from 40/35. Germany (our defensive pact ally) is proposing Sphere of Influence on Salem and has 211 influence vs our -55. We cannot win this contest and should not compete with our only major ally. Preserving German alliance is critical for survival against France. Let Germany secure Salem permanently.
        - UpdatedTurn: 306
        
        ### China
        - Public: -25
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: CHINA HOSTILITY REDUCED: From -30/-35 to -25/-30. China effectively neutralized - 1 city (Macau), 47 military strength, 100% war weariness, -100% war score vs Germany. They pose zero threat. Further reduction acknowledges their defeat while maintaining negative stance due to technical war state.
        - UpdatedTurn: 340
        
        ### Siam
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 35
        - Rationale: SIAM VASSAL STABILITY: Vassal provides 9 delegates supporting World Congress interests. Recent Zoroastrian conversion at Nakhon Si Thammarat strengthens ideological bond. Their 699 military strength and diplomatic alignment (Friendship with Germany) supports coalition geography. Content with our mastery - stable for war support.
        - UpdatedTurn: 359
        
        ### Enns
        - Public: 60
        - Private: 55
        - Rationale: ENNS RECOVERY MODE: German coup succeeded - we dropped from 69 to neutral. Germany now has 33 influence, we have minimal. Moderate positive stance (60/55) preserves quest opportunities and signals to Germany this was not acceptable, while avoiding full rupture. Not worth heavy gold investment given German proximity advantage - consolidate on Kyzyl/Lisbon defense instead.
        - UpdatedTurn: 376
        
        ### Indonesia
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -15
        - Rationale: INDONESIA VASSAL NEUTRALITY: German vassal status confirmed (Turn 374). Previous -25/-30 stance created tension with Germany (defensive pact ally). Adjusting to minimal negativity acknowledges Germany's sphere without endorsing Indonesian actions. Avoid accidental conflicts with Germany while maintaining watch on former enemy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 376
        
        ### Marrakech
        - Public: 45
        - Private: 40
        - Rationale: MARRAKECH ACCEPT REALITY: Adjust from 40/35 to 45/40. Germany's 1026 influence vs our 477 makes contest unwinnable. Agent Adapa still rigging there but resources better spent on Lisbon/Kyzyl defense given Decolonization threat. Minor positive adjustment acknowledges reality without endorsing German coup. Preserve quest opportunities and diplomatic flexibility but concede delegate to focus on existential priorities.
        - UpdatedTurn: 384
        
        ### Japan
        - Public: -95
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: JAPAN MAXIMUM HOSTILITY: Maintain -95/-100. French vassal with 100% war weariness, effectively defeated. Their 6 delegates still vote with France in World Congress. They must be eliminated to reduce French voting bloc. Coordinate military action with anti-French coalition when opportunity permits.
        - UpdatedTurn: 388
        
        ### France
        - Public: -95
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: FRANCE MAXIMUM HOSTILITY MAINTAINED: French delegate count (27/44, 61%) remains existential threat. Their military 12,696 vs our 4,474 requires total war stance. Voting FOR Decolonization will cripple their diplomatic victory - this is our best chance to stop them without direct confrontation. Maintain hostile posture to signal intent to block their victory at all costs.
        - UpdatedTurn: 389
        
        ### Germany
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: GERMANY MAXIMUM HOSTILITY - BETRAYAL RESPONSE: Germany's unprovoked declaration of war (despite defensive pact, shared Order ideology, and coordinated anti-French strategy) is existential betrayal. They plundered 5 trade routes, killed civilian units, and invaded my territory. Total war stance required - they are now primary enemy alongside France. No diplomatic solution possible while they control 13 delegates and 13,313 military strength. Must fight to survive.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### The Inca
        - Public: 55
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: INCA RELATIONSHIP REDUCED - DECOLONIZATION COLLATERAL DAMAGE: The Inca's Decolonization proposal passed and directly cost me 2 delegates (Lisbon/Kyzyl/Florence reduced to 50 influence). While they remain my only defensive pact ally and provide +999 Order ideology bonus, their proposal crippled my diplomatic position. Reducing from 65/60 to 55/50 signals strong displeasure while maintaining alliance necessity against Germany/France dual threat. Complex diplomatic balance - need them but cannot forgive the delegate loss.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### Lisbon
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: LISBON RECOVERY PRIORITY: Decolonization reduced influence from 595 to 50 (neutral). Lisbon provides 2 delegates critical for diplomatic survival. With 10 turns until next World Congress vote, must prioritize gold investment and quests to rebuild alliance. 100/100 relationship ensures AI commits maximum resources to influence recovery. Target: Regain ally status within 8 turns.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### Kyzyl
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: KYZYL RECOVERY PRIORITY: Decolonization reduced influence from 682 to 50 (neutral). Kyzyl provides 2 delegates and Agent Nukimmut is rigging elections there. 100/100 relationship ensures maximum investment to rebuild alliance. Combined with Lisbon represents 4 delegates - essential for maintaining diplomatic relevance. Target: Regain ally status within 8 turns before next World Congress.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### Florence
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: FLORENCE RECOVERY PRIORITY: Decolonization reduced influence from 158 to 50 (lost ally status same turn gained it). Florence provides delegate and Agent Asnappar rigging there. 100/100 relationship prioritizes rebuilding this alliance despite competing with Japan and Germany. Every delegate counts when facing 24-delegate France.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: TOTAL WAR ECONOMY - DUAL THREAT RESPONSE: Maximum Defense 100 and Antiair 100 to survive Germany+France onslaught. Science increased to 100 - nuclear weapons are existential priority. Nuke/UseNuke at 100/90 for immediate deterrent upon reaching Atomic Theory. Espionage and Diplomacy at 100 to rig city-state elections (Lisbon/Kyzyl/Florence) and recover lost delegates from Decolonization. Gold reduced to 50 - economy devastated by trade route plundering. Production/Mobilization at 100 for military infrastructure. Survival through superior technology and deterrence.
        - GrandStrategy: Conquest

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 44
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 9 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Open Door: Enns:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: 367
          - World Religion: Catholicism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - German Sphere of Influence: Colombo:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: 356
          - French Sphere of Influence: Heidabir:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - French Sphere of Influence: Edinburgh:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: France:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: 345
        - Proposals:

The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.

        You, Ashurbanipal (leader of Assyria, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 391.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Rocketry: Allows you to build the Guided Missile, a one-time use unit specialized in damaging City garrisons.
        Completing it would unlock: Nuclear Fission, Radar
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Penicillin: All Workers receive the Fallout Reduction Promotion, which reduces the amount of damage they take while scrubbing Fallout. Allows you to build the Marine, a Unit that specializes in amphibious assaults. Also allows you to build the Medical Lab, a building which speeds the growth of your Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 4
        - Public: 80
        - Rationale: The Inca is our crucial Defensive Pact partner and ideological ally (+999 Order bonus). As we fight an existential defensive war against the dual threat of Germany and France, we must maximize our relations and ensure full military and economic alignment.
        - Private: 80
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 3
        - Public: 60
        - Rationale: Japan is no longer a French vassal, is at war with France, and is our World Congress ally. We are raising our relationship to friendly to reflect this mutual alliance against France and secure their ongoing diplomatic support.
        - Private: 60
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 29
        - Public: 0
        - Rationale: Lisbon is a German ally and has declared war on us on Turn 391. Since we are at war and cannot build influence, we reset our relationship to neutral to stop the AI from wasting valuable diplomatic resources.
        - Private: 0
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Marrakech is allied with our friend Japan. We should avoid competing with Japan for influence here and instead focus our espionage and diplomacy on maintaining our alliances with Kyzyl and Florence.
        - TargetID: 35
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Rocketry
        - Rationale: Rocketry is the prerequisite for Nuclear Fission, which unlocks Atomic Bombs. In our desperate existential defense against Germany's massive army, our only hope for long-term survival is establishing a strong nuclear deterrent.
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Order Level 2 tenet provides 5 free Research Labs and +100% production to remaining labs. This will massively boost our science output to reach Atomic Theory and Rocketry faster, while saving thousands of production that our cities can instead use to build defensive military units.
        - Policy: Academy of Sciences
    - #6:
      - toolName: keep-status-quo
      - rationale: Our current Conquest grand strategy and highly defensive flavors (Defense 100, Mobilization 100, Nuke 100, Air 100, Antiair 100) are perfectly aligned with our total war survival strategy. We will maintain these settings as we defend our cities and push towards nuclear weapons.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Current Game State**
            
            I'm currently reviewing the Civilization V game at turn 391, playing as Ashurbanipal of Assyria. My focus is on analyzing the current geopolitical and technological landscape to formulate the most effective next strategic moves.
            
            **Refining Tech Path**
            
            I'm re-evaluating my technology trajectory. While Atomic Theory is nearing completion, I'm exploring the next logical advancements from Rocketry, Refrigeration, and Penicillin to best leverage my current position. My policy selection is also under review.
            
            **Choosing Ideology Tenets**
            
            I'm prioritizing which Ideology tenets to pursue next. The options from Order are extensive, and I'm weighing the benefits of tenets like Dictatorship of the Proletariat and Five-Year Plan against other branches.
            
            **Evaluating Ideology Options**
            
            My previous thought on Ideology tenets has taken a sharp turn. Given Germany's recent surprise attack and trade route plundering, the path of Order, specifically tenets like "Academy of Sciences," seems less prudent. I must reconsider my immediate Ideology choices and explore how to best counter this aggression.
            
            **Assessing Global Conflicts**
            
            I'm reassessing our current war footing. It seems I've underestimated the extent of our conflicts; we are now at war with Germany, France, Indonesia, and several city-states, including Lisbon, Sokoto, and Buenos Aires. Even our ally, The Inca, is only at war with France, not Germany. This requires an immediate strategic pivot.
            
            **Investigating Defensive Pact Mechanics**
            
            I'm trying to understand why our defensive pact with the Inca didn't trigger a war against Germany. It appears I need to revisit the exact conditions under which these pacts activate. Recent events show intense German aggression on turn 391, with multiple units attacking our cities.
            
            **Revising Unit Deployment**
            
            My previous assessment of the global conflicts needs immediate revision. We've suffered significant unit losses, particularly to a concentrated German offensive. I'm now re-evaluating my unit deployment, focusing on defending our core cities against this overwhelming land and sea assault, while also noting our new alliances with Florence and Kyzyl.
            
            **Realigning Alliances**
            
            My focus has shifted dramatically. We've successfully re-established an alliance with Kyzyl, which is a small comfort. However, Lisbon, Salem, Colombo, Sokoto, and Buenos Aires have all declared war against us, aligned with Germany. This exacerbates our dire situation, leaving us facing overwhelming odds against Germany and France.
            
            **Analyzing Enemy Proximity**
            
            I'm re-examining the map and enemy city placements. German cities like Leipzig and Xian are alarmingly close to our core territories, and Shanghai and Nanjing are under severe siege. This close proximity demands an immediate shift in defensive strategy.
            
            **Prioritizing City Defense**
            
            I'm intensely focused on shoring up defenses for Nanjing and Shanghai against overwhelming German assaults. My immediate priority is maximizing military production and research for better weaponry to ensure survival against this relentless, multi-pronged attack.
            
            **Prioritizing Rocketry Research**
            
            I'm focusing my immediate research efforts on Rocketry. This advancement is crucial as it unlocks Nuclear Fission, a necessary step towards developing atomic weapons. I'm also reviewing our next policy choices to best support our strategic objectives.
            
            **Prioritizing Science Investment**
            
            I'm reconsidering my immediate Ideology choices. While "Patriotic War" offers tactical combat bonuses, the immense science boost from "Academy of Sciences" feels far more critical. The five free Research Labs will significantly accelerate my path towards Rocketry and future atomic capabilities, which seems a more pressing concern given the current global situation.
            
            **Choosing Ideology Tenets**
            
            I'm re-evaluating my ideology choices. While defensive bonuses are appealing, a free technology from "Great Leap Forward" or accelerated science from "Academy of Sciences" seems more crucial for long-term survival and counter-offensives. The current war situation demands a strategic pivot towards rapid technological advancement.
            
            **Comparing Ideology Bonuses**
            
            I'm weighing the immediate impact of a free technology from "Great Leap Forward" against the long-term science boost from five free Research Labs via "Academy of Sciences." While a free tech could instantly finish Atomic Theory or Flight, the substantial, permanent science gain from the labs seems more critical for achieving future atomic capabilities and overall survival against overwhelming odds.
            
            **Prioritizing Immediate Defense**
            
            My current focus is on immediate tactical advantage. While science is important, the severe threat to Nanjing and Shanghai demands prioritizing "Patriotic War" for its defensive combat bonuses. This tenet seems critical for preserving our cities against overwhelming odds, even if it means delaying extensive science investment.
            
            **Choosing Ideology Focus**
            
            I am prioritizing immediate defense. "Patriotic War" offers crucial combat bonuses for my beleaguered cities, outweighing the long-term science gains from "Academy of Sciences" or the immediate tech from "Great Leap Forward" in my current dire situation.
            
            **Prioritizing Atomic Development**
            
            My thoughts are now laser-focused on accelerating towards nuclear capabilities. The sheer military disparity with Germany and France necessitates asymmetric warfare. Maximizing science to reach Atomic Theory, Rocketry, and Nuclear Fission is paramount. "Academy of Sciences" with its free labs offers a significant, sustained science boost, which I'm comparing against the immediate advantage of a free tech from "Great Leap Forward."
            
            **Calculating Science Advantage**
            
            My focus has shifted to quantifying the benefits of "Academy of Sciences." The five free Research Labs offer a significant science boost of 150-200 per turn, accelerating my path to Atomic Theory by an estimated 20 turns. Crucially, this also frees up substantial production time, allowing me to prioritize military unit construction for immediate defense.
            
            **Selecting Ideology Tenets**
            
            I've decided to pursue the "Academy of Sciences" ideology tenet. The significant science boost and saved production are crucial for accelerating my path to atomic capabilities and building more units, outweighing immediate tactical advantages or single-tech boosts. I'm also focusing on improving my relationship with the Inca to solidify our alliance.
            
            **Revising Diplomatic Standing**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our relationship with Japan. I discovered our recent diplomatic standing was drastically negative, a result of past actions when they were a French vassal. Now that Japan is independent, I need to adjust our strategy and mend this relationship to potentially secure a new ally.
            
            **Mending Diplomatic Ties**
            
            My previous diplomatic assessment of Japan was based on outdated information. Now that they've broken free from France and are actively warring against them, and share a defensive pact with our ally the Inca, I must immediately correct our relationship to positive. This presents a critical opportunity for a new, valuable alliance.
            
            **Strengthening Key Alliances**
            
            I'm focusing on consolidating my alliances. Japan is now a valuable friend and a strong partner against Germany and France. I've also successfully re-established our alliance with Kyzyl, ensuring we maintain positive relations and their critical support in ongoing conflicts.
            
            **Securing City-State Alliances**
            
            I'm prioritizing solidifying alliances with key city-states. Florence, allied with us and at war with our enemies, is crucial. I must maintain our influence there. Marrakech's situation is complex; Japan, our ally, is allied there, and our spy failed to rig elections. I will ensure we don't contest Japan's influence there.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Balances**
            
            I'm fine-tuning our city-state relationships. I'll adjust Marrakech to a neutral stance to avoid conflict with our ally Japan. Enns, a religious ally with high influence, will be maintained at a moderate friendly level. I'm also reviewing our standing with major powers, confirming war with Germany and France while solidifying alliances with the Inca and Japan.
            
            **Optimizing City-State Influence**
            
            I'm resetting my influence with Lisbon, Colombo, Salem, Sokoto, and Buenos Aires to zero, as they are at war with us. Florence and Kyzyl will remain at maximum influence as allies. Marrakech will be reset to zero to avoid competition with our ally Japan.
            
            **Prioritizing Production Allocation**
            
            I'm reassessing my city production queues. The current focus on non-military buildings like Broadcast Towers and Hotels is nonsensical given the overwhelming German invasion. I must immediately shift all city production towards military units to bolster our defenses against this existential threat.
            
            **Adjusting Production Focus**
            
            My city production queues are being aggressively revised. The current non-military projects are illogical given the existential threat, so all cities will immediately pivot to military unit construction. This is further reinforced by ensuring no city gets stuck on infinite projects by adjusting flavors, though they are already highly optimized for defense and production.
            
            **Refining Defensive Flavors**
            
            My current focus is on optimizing the AI's flavor values for total war defense and accelerated science. The existing flavors are excellent, particularly for defense and production, with a strong emphasis on science. I'm now reviewing persona values to ensure they align with this defensive and scientific posture.
            
            **Setting Persona Values**
            
            My persona values are currently set to be highly defensive and competitive, perfectly aligning with our total war strategy. I see no need to adjust them at this time. I'll proceed with setting our research to Rocketry and updating relationships with Japan and Lisbon.
            
            **Defending Strategic Sectors**
            
            My focus is on the immediate battle for Shanghai and Nanjing. We've secured key victories against German and Lisbon units, showcasing effective tactical responses. I'm now reinforcing these sectors to hold against the sustained enemy pressure, especially considering the loss of our Citadel near Shanghai.
            
            **Analyzing Unit Strength Discrepancies**
            
            I'm investigating why our reported friendly strength in Nanjing seems low, especially given the number of infantry units present. I suspect some units are heavily damaged and healing, impacting the aggregate strength calculation. I'm also noting positive developments in naval engagements, like destroying a French submarine near Indonesia's capital.
            
            **Capturing Key Cities**
            
            I'm prioritizing the capture of Samarinda. It's at critical health, and as Germany's vassal, it's now a direct target. My Destroyer can secure it, gaining a new city and advancing my strategic goals. This city's capture is a top priority.
            
            **Securing Opportunistic Conquest**
            
            I'm considering an opportunistic capture of Samarinda, Indonesia's only city. Given Gajah Mada's Modern Era status and our technological parity, I anticipate a significant science bonus upon conquest. This move would also eliminate Indonesia from the game, offering a strategic advantage. My destroyers are positioned for the assault.
            
            **Strengthening Crucial Alliances**
            
            I'm prioritizing the Inca. Setting their relationship to 80 Public and 80 Private is essential, as they are our sole defensive pact ally and share our Order ideology during this critical war against Germany and France.
            
            **Updating Diplomatic Standing**
            
            I am meticulously revising our diplomatic standing with several factions. Japan's relationship is being elevated due to their new independence and alliance against France. Lisbon's standing is reset to zero, reflecting their declaration of war and alliance with Germany. Marrakech will also be reset to neutral to avoid conflict with our ally Japan.
            
            **Shifting Grand Strategy**
            
            My previous decision to maintain a "Conquest" grand strategy is no longer appropriate. Given the overwhelming odds and existential threat from Germany and France, a shift to a defensive, survival-focused approach is imperative. My focus is now on securing crucial alliances and accelerating our scientific development to achieve a nuclear deterrent.
            
            **Maintaining Current Strategy**
            
            My thought process confirms that our current AI flavor settings are perfectly optimized for total war defense, rapid mobilization, production, science, and nuclear development. Thus, I'm opting to maintain the status quo, documenting this rationale.
            
            **Clarifying Diplomatic Tools**
            
            I'm refining my understanding of the `set-relationship` tool. It's specifically for major, non-city-state civilizations, meaning I can't directly use it on city-states like Lisbon or Marrakech. I need to find alternative methods to manage my standing with them.
            
            **Clarifying City-State Interactions**
            
            I'm delving deeper into the `set-relationship` tool, specifically its application to city-states. While the description mentions "MAJOR (non-city-state) civilizations," I'm investigating if the underlying mechanics allow me to adjust relationships with city-states via their Opponent IDs, as these modifiers influence their AI behavior towards me.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Engagements**
            
            I am now focusing on precisely implementing diplomatic adjustments. The `set-relationship` tool is confirmed to function with city-states via their Opponent IDs. Therefore, I will set The Inca to 80 Public and 80 Private, Japan to 60 Public and 60 Private, and reset Lisbon and Marrakech to 0 Public and 0 Private to reflect current strategic priorities.
            
            **Finalizing Diplomatic Actions**
            
            I'm meticulously finalizing all diplomatic settings. The Inca and Japan's relationships are set to reflect their alliance strength, while Lisbon and Marrakech are reset to neutral to align with current strategic needs. China is confirmed defeated. My research is focused on Rocketry and "Academy of Sciences" policy is selected.
            
            **Finalizing Diplomatic Actions**
            
            I'm meticulously finalizing all diplomatic settings. The Inca and Japan's relationships are set to reflect their alliance strength, while Lisbon and Marrakech are reset to neutral to align with current strategic needs. China is confirmed defeated. My research is focused on Rocketry and "Academy of Sciences" policy is selected.

          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - TargetID: 3
            - Public: 60
            - Rationale: Japan is no longer a French vassal, is at war with France, and is our World Congress ally. We are raising our relationship to friendly to reflect this mutual alliance against France and secure their ongoing diplomatic support.
            - Private: 60

          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: 4DTaqEoY8x5khDVU
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - TargetID: 29
            - Public: 0
            - Rationale: Lisbon is a German ally and has declared war on us on Turn 391. Since we are at war and cannot build influence, we reset our relationship to neutral to stop the AI from wasting valuable diplomatic resources.
            - Private: 0
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #4:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: oQ2n5GZxfRC5EmMZ
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Rationale: Marrakech is allied with our friend Japan. We should avoid competing with Japan for influence here and instead focus our espionage and diplomacy on maintaining our alliances with Kyzyl and Florence.
            - TargetID: 35
            - Public: 0
            - Private: 0
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #5:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: YEssCANZSszZzGT1
          - toolName: set-research
          - input:
            - Technology: Rocketry
            - Rationale: Rocketry is the prerequisite for Nuclear Fission, which unlocks Atomic Bombs. In our desperate existential defense against Germany's massive army, our only hope for long-term survival is establishing a strong nuclear deterrent.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #6:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: DBhnFU5YlkHBOykk
          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Rationale: Order Level 2 tenet provides 5 free Research Labs and +100% production to remaining labs. This will massively boost our science output to reach Atomic Theory and Rocketry faster, while saving thousands of production that our cities can instead use to build defensive military units.
            - Policy: Academy of Sciences
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #7:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: qUMA9FvjtRzyFd63
          - toolName: keep-status-quo
          - input:
            - Rationale: Our current Conquest grand strategy and highly defensive flavors (Defense 100, Mobilization 100, Nuke 100, Air 100, Antiair 100) are perfectly aligned with our total war survival strategy. We will maintain these settings as we defend our cities and push towards nuclear weapons.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
